Abstract: Recent advances in robotics research have created a strong demand for high-performance simulators. Surgical robotics simulation faces unique challenges due to the need to model diverse objects, such as rigid instruments, soft tissue, and fluids. While many studies simulate sutures or soft tissue independently, only a few have considered the complete soft-tissue suturing scenario, including the contact between sutures and deformable tissue during suture insertion. Building on previous work, this paper presents a novel suturing simulation environment using sutures modelled by position-based dynamics (PBD) and soft bodies modelled by the material point method (MPM) while considering two-way contact with frictional and drag forces. We introduce a contact coupling method between the PBD suture and the MPM soft tissue, enabling visually plausible suture-tissue interactions. The simulator is optimized for GPU execution with parallel scenes using multiple CUDA streams, and we present a Reinforcement Learning (RL) environment for autonomous suturing sub-tasks, including needle insertion, driving, and extraction. Using ML-Agents, RL agents trained in the simulator show stable learning and achieve 80% and 68% success rates in needle insertion and extraction, respectively, under the strictest distance threshold.

</details>


### [294] [Corrigible Assistance in One Round: Pragmatic-Pedagogic Best Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27508)
*Elle Lazarski,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文识别了一类可在单步内通过实用-教学推理解决目标不确定性的协助博弈，并证明其可精确求解；同时指出主流逆最优控制存在推理上限，而所提方法能通过即时消歧目标来克服。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协助博弈中在线计算最优策略通常因POMDP规划而难以处理，需要寻找可精确求解的特定类别，并克服现有逆最优控制方法的对齐限制。

Method: 定义一类协助博弈，其中智能体通过实用-教学推理（pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning）在单时间步内消除目标不确定性，从而将完整时域问题简化为可处理的best-response过程。

Result: 证明了该类别问题可精确求解；主流逆最优控制存在推理上限，而实用-教学推理能立即消歧目标；在简单协作积木搭建任务上验证了理论结果和方法。

Conclusion: 实用-教学推理能有效解决特定协助博弈中的对齐问题，为高维或复杂场景提供可求解策略，未来可扩展至更广泛的人机协作设置。

Abstract: Assistance games formalize human-robot collaboration under asymmetric information: the human knows the goal, while the robot must infer it from observation and interaction in order to assist effectively. In general, computing optimal assistance game strategies online is intractable, since exact solutions require planning in a POMDP. We identify a class of assistance games in which pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning resolves goal uncertainty in a single time step, rendering the full-horizon game exactly solvable by a tractable best-response procedure. Within this class, we show that mainstream inverse optimal control exhibits an inference ceiling that hinders alignment, while pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning overcomes this barrier by immediately disambiguating goals through actions that look equivalent under task execution alone. Finally, we validate our theoretical results and proposed method on a simple collaborative block-building example.

</details>


### [295] [Failure Detection for Surgical Robot Imitation Policies via Flow-Matching World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27511)
*Zhefeng Huang,Yilin Cai,Ankit Patel,Mohammad Hajiha,Brendan Browne,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FoMo-FD，一种基于流匹配世界模型的失败检测方法，用于机器人手术中的视觉-动作不一致检测，无需失败演示即可实现高检测率和低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手术中模仿学习的安全部署面临挑战，而失败检测因失败数据稀缺、动力学变化大以及检测与误报的权衡而难以开发。

Method: FoMo-FD学习正常短期视觉动力学的动作条件流匹配世界模型，通过逆传输非一致性评分观察终点潜在变量，并通过共形校准获得检测阈值。

Result: 在四个手术相关操作任务和二十种失败模式上，FoMo-FD优于基线方法，在腕部相机视角下达到96.6%的失败检测率（FDR）和1.3%的误报率（FAR）。

Conclusion: FoMo-FD有效检测手术中的视觉-动作不一致，无需失败数据即可实现鲁棒失败检测，且腕部相机视角性能最佳。

Abstract: Imitation learning has shown increasing promise for autonomous robotic surgery, yet safe deployment remains challenging due to the safety-critical nature of surgical tasks and the complexity and variability of surgical environments. Failure detection is therefore an essential safeguard, but its development remains difficult due to the challenges of scarce failure data, highly variable manipulation dynamics, and the need to balance missed detections against disruptive false alarms. To address these challenges, we introduce FoMo-FD (Flow-Matching World Model for Failure Detection), a failure detection method that learns nominal short-horizon visual dynamics with an action-conditioned flow-matching world model. FoMo-FD scores the inverse-transport nonconformity of observed endpoint latents, enabling window-level detection of visual-action inconsistencies without requiring failure demonstrations. Detection thresholds are obtained by conformal calibration on successful executions, yielding task-specific alarms without assuming future failure types. We evaluate FoMo-FD on four surgically relevant manipulation tasks with twenty failure modes across simulation and real-world experiments using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). Results show that FoMo-FD outperforms observation-level anomaly baselines and a prediction-error variant of the same world model, with the wrist-camera view achieving the strongest performance, including a 96.6% failure detection rate (FDR) at a 1.3% false alarm rate (FAR).

</details>


### [296] [Cross-Embodiment Transfer via Behavior-Aligned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27549)
*Ajay Sridhar,Jensen Gao,Jonathan Yang,Jean Mercat,Suneel Belkhale,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为对齐表示（如物体边界框、语言运动、末端执行器轨迹）在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中对跨本体迁移的作用，发现末端执行器轨迹特别有益，且表示在更大数据集和仿真到真实迁移中能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模仿学习虽在机器人操作中取得进展，但跨本体迁移仍具挑战。作者假设行为对齐表示因具有跨本体的不变性且预测动作，可统一跨本体数据以增强迁移。

Method: 构建基于仿真的基准评估不同表示及融合方式，比较多种表示（如边界框、语言运动、末端轨迹），并测试其在大数据集、无动作数据及仿真到真实迁移中的效果。

Result: 末端执行器轨迹对迁移最有效；表示在大数据集下更有用，可利用无动作数据，并在仿真到真实的跨本体迁移中提升真实策略任务完成进度28%。

Conclusion: 行为对齐表示，尤其末端执行器轨迹，能有效促进跨本体迁移，为多本体机器人学习提供新方向。

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale imitation learning for robot manipulation has been driven by leveraging datasets across a wide range of robot embodiments. However, achieving significant cross-embodiment transfer is often still challenging. In this work, we study the role of using behavior-aligned representations (e.g., object bounding boxes, language motions, end-effector traces of robot motion) in vision-language-action (VLA) models to promote cross-embodiment transfer. We hypothesize that by possessing invariances across embodiments while being predictive of robot actions, these representations can help unify large-scale cross-embodiment data to enhance transfer. To assess our hypothesis, we develop a simulation-based benchmark designed to assess transfer with diverse cross-embodiment data to new embodiments. Using this benchmark, we compare different representations and ways of incorporating them. We identify that end-effector traces can be particularly beneficial for transfer, representations are generally more useful with larger prior datasets, and can be used to benefit from action-free data. We also demonstrate that they can enhance sim-to-real cross-embodiment transfer, improving task completion progress of real robot policies pre-trained on simulation data by 28%. We provide videos of our evaluations at our website: https://ajaysridhar.com/barx/.

</details>


### [297] [A Systems Engineering Framework for Vision-Language-Enabled UAV Triage and Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27597)
*Swapnil Saha,Bhuvan Rajanasiriyur Jagadeesha,Karishma Patnaik,Neelakshi Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了将视觉语言模型嵌入无人机协调代理的可行性，以支持灾害响应中的人机协作，通过自然语言交互和任务协调减轻操作员负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM应用限于决策支持，增加操作员负担，因为人类仍需将输出转化为团队和机器人的协调行动。

Method: 采用基于模型的系统工程方法，开发了包含VLM协调代理、无人机任务控制和任务分配器的架构，并通过软件在环模拟和七名参与者的人类因素评估进行测试。

Result: 初步评估显示感知工作量降低（心理需求、努力和挫折感），AI信任和沟通清晰度评分较高。

Conclusion: 通过集成MBSE、软件在环测试和人类因素评估，该方法推进了高风险灾害响应中的可扩展人机协同，对航空航天自主和民事安全具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have created new opportunities for disaster response, where responders must interpret large volumes of sensor data under time pressure. Current VLM applications include social media monitoring for situational awareness, generation of draft action plans, and translation of technical alerts into public-facing messages. While these efforts can accelerate information flow, they remain largely limited to decision-support roles. Such approaches can increase operator burden because humans must still translate outputs into coordinated actions across teams and robotic assets. This study explores the viability of embedding VLMs as coordination agents within the human-UAV loop. The proposed architecture integrates natural language interaction, mission-level task coordination, software-in-the-loop implementation, and communication aligned with the Incident Command System (ICS). Rather than functioning solely as advisory tools, VLMs facilitate communication between human operators, mission control logic, and UAV task execution. The framework was developed using a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) approach, with use case and block definition diagrams representing system roles, internal structure, and component interactions. Three key elements, the VLM Coordinator Agent, UAV Mission Control, and Task Allocator, were implemented within an integrated simulation and control environment. A preliminary human-factors evaluation with seven participants showed reduced perceived workload across mental demand, effort, and frustration, along with high ratings for AI trust and communication clarity. By integrating MBSE, software-in-the-loop testing, and human-factors evaluation, this work advances scalable human-autonomy teaming for high-stakes disaster response, with broader implications for aerospace autonomy and civil safety.

</details>


### [298] [Arm2Air: Cross-Embodiment Skeleton Transfer for 3D Relay Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27627)
*Dohun Lee,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Seokmin Kim,Byongho Lee,Seungjoo Oh,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Arm2Air方法，通过跨具身迁移将机械臂避障骨架用于无人机中继部署，在三维城市障碍环境中显著提升规划效率、容量和鲁棒性，并降低数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机中继网络在通信基础设施受损后恢复连接至关重要，但城市环境中中继部署需同时考虑视距遮挡、通信范围、高度和三维障碍，现有方法计算昂贵且数据低效。

Method: 利用预训练机械臂运动模型的避障骨架，通过跨具身迁移转换为有序骨架，预训练Transformer迁移平台，并用目标域数据通过低秩适配（LoRA）微调。迁移的骨架初始化中继链，然后优化连接性、瓶颈容量、延迟和移动成本。

Result: 在9张高杂乱3D城市地图上，规划运行时间中位数减少64.9%；在30张密集城市地图的高遮挡子集上，相对IMPC-MD，瓶颈容量提升32.6%，容量方差降低74.7%，最大跳距减少13.2%，跳距方差降低75.2%，中继位移减少16.9%。仅用3张目标域训练图，位置RMSE降低53.6%，仅更新0.134M参数，而从头训练需1.383M。

Conclusion: Arm2Air展示了跨具身迁移有序结构先验的可行性，实现计算和数据高效的无人机中继部署，并可能推广到其他异构具身任务。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) relay networks can restore connectivity after communication infrastructure is damaged. Urban relay placement is difficult because line-of-sight blockage, communication range, altitude, and three-dimensional obstacles must be considered jointly. Arm2Air transfers obstacle-avoidance skeletons from robot arms to UAV relay placement through cross-embodiment transfer. Source-domain robot-arm motions from a pretrained Neural MP model are converted into ordered skeletons that pretrain a transformer-based transfer platform, which is then adapted to the UAV domain using limited target data and Low-Rank Adaptation. The transferred skeleton initializes a relay chain that is refined for connectivity, bottleneck capacity, delay, and movement cost. On nine held-out high-clutter 3D urban maps, Arm2Air reduced median end-to-end planning runtime by 64.9 percent relative to the fastest conventional planner. On the high-obstruction group of a separate 30-map dense urban holdout, it increased bottleneck capacity by 32.6 percent, reduced capacity variance by 74.7 percent, reduced maximum hop distance by 13.2 percent, reduced hop-distance variance by 75.2 percent, and reduced relay displacement by 16.9 percent relative to IMPC-MD. With only three target-domain training maps, Arm2Air reduced relay-position root mean square error by 53.6 percent relative to training from scratch while updating 0.134 million parameters, compared with 1.383 million for Scratch and Full Fine-tuning. These results demonstrate computationally and data-efficient UAV relay placement and suggest a broader principle for transferring ordered structural priors across heterogeneous embodied tasks.

</details>


### [299] [LabEvolver: Training-Free Experience Evolution for Safe and Grounded Wet-Lab Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27690)
*Jingya Wang,Yuyang Gao,Liuzhenghao Lv,Yonghong Tian,Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LabEvolver，一个无需训练的框架，通过执行经验为湿实验室代理配备情景记忆，显著提升任务效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化科学发现代理缺乏从经验中学习的能力，难以适应复杂、动态的实验室环境，导致效率低下和安全风险。

Method: LabEvolver采用双环结构：内环进行状态引导的自适应感知、在线规划和安全性验证；外环将完成的轨迹蒸馏为可复用的技能、策略和安全经验。

Result: 在机器人溶液制备任务中，pH调节完成时间减少48.2%，安全门拦截减少60.0%；在ALFWorld上，20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升至91.4%（500个连续任务），证明其跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 边做边学的经验演化是实现闭环自动化科学发现的有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce LabEvolver, a training-free framework that equips safe and grounded wet-lab agents with episodic memory from execution experience. LabEvolver couples a state-grounded inner trial loop for adaptive perception, online planning, and safety validation with an outer evolution loop that distills completed trajectories into reusable skill, strategy, and safety experience. On robotic solution-preparation tasks, LabEvolver demonstrates real-world feasibility, reducing pH-regulation completion time and safety-gate intercepts by 48.2% and 60.0%, respectively. On ALFWorld, it further improves cumulative success rate within 20 steps from 76.2% with ReAct to 91.4% over 500 continual tasks, showing generality beyond wet-lab settings. These results support learn-by-doing experience evolution as a feasible path toward closed-loop automated scientific discovery. The project page is available at https://github.com/AndyGao6186/LabEvolver.

</details>


### [300] [Write-Safe Flow Field Mapping under Ambiguous Onboard Sensing and Localization Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27713)
*Linhao Jin,Qimin Feng,Peter Gunnarson,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种保守融合框架，通过学习写入安全分数来减少移动机器人流场建图中的幽灵结构，实验显示污染降低42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人从本地传感推断的流场结构写入全局地图时，可能因观测模糊或定位漂移导致错误写入，累积形成幽灵结构。

Method: 预测局部速度场和可学习的写入安全分数，该分数持续衰减不确定的更新，但允许在无可靠地图参考时初始化。

Result: 合成射流和横流环境中平均幽灵污染降低42%；使用真实数据的零样本硬件回放中污染降低39%，同时保留81%地图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 安全的地图写入对于在模糊传感和定位漂移条件下进行流场建图至关重要。

Abstract: Mobile robots can infer local flow structure from onboard sensing, but a locally plausible estimate is not always safe to write into a global map. Similar flow structures may produce ambiguous observations, while localization drift causes predicted patches to be written at incorrect locations. Repeated misregistered updates then accumulate into persistent ghost structures. We address this failure mode with a map-reference-aware conservative fusion framework. The model predicts a local velocity patch and a learned write-safety score that continuously attenuates uncertain map updates while permitting initialization when no reliable map reference is available. Across synthetic jet and crossflow environments, the proposed method reduces average ghost contamination by 42% relative to ungated fusion. A zero-shot hardware replay using real pressure and optical-flow measurements from a thruster wake further reduces ghost contamination by 39% while retaining 81% map coverage. These results show that safe map writing is critical for flow mapping under ambiguous sensing and localization drift.

</details>


### [301] [RedFlow: Redirect Failure into Action-Level Corrections for Flow-matching VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27782)
*Zhengyang Yan,Junhao Li,Fangqi Zhu,Zijun Wang,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Xiaoyi Pang,Zicong Hong,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RedFlow，一种细粒度的离线强化学习框架，通过将失败经验转化为动作级别的纠正监督，提升流匹配VLA策略在机器人操作中的鲁棒性，显著降低复合错误并提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA策略在部署时因分布偏移导致复合错误，现有离线RL方法要么忽略失败数据，要么仅在轨迹级别利用，导致学习效率低和错误持续存在。

Method: RedFlow包含两个关键组件：上下文感知纠正匹配（识别导致失败的动作并从相似上下文检索成功动作作为纠正目标）和自适应重定向目标（联合强化成功动作、抑制不良动作、将可恢复的失败重定向到纠正目标），从而将成功和失败经验转化为密集监督信号。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和三个真实世界操作任务中，RedFlow一致优于最先进的离线RL基线，真实世界成功率从56.7%提升至74.7%，并匹配强在线方法（PPO、GRPO、DDPO）的性能，同时训练样本减少约一个数量级。

Conclusion: RedFlow通过细粒度的纠正监督有效利用混合质量数据，实现鲁棒的恢复学习，为离线RL与VLA策略的结合提供了一种高效方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation but often suffer from compounding errors caused by distribution shifts during deployment. While offline reinforcement learning (RL) provides a practical way to improve deployed policies using rollout data, existing methods either ignore failure data or exploit it only at the trajectory level, resulting in low learning efficiency and persistent errors. We propose **RedFlow**, a fine-grained offline RL framework that redirects failure experiences into action-level corrective supervision for flow-matching VLA policies. RedFlow consists of two key components: (1) a **Context-Aware Corrective Matching** mechanism that identifies failure-inducing actions and retrieves successful alternatives from similar contexts as corrective targets, and (2) an **Adaptive Redirection Objective** that jointly reinforces successful actions, suppresses undesirable ones, and redirects recoverable failures toward corrective targets. By converting both successful and failed experiences into dense supervision, RedFlow enables robust recovery learning from mixed-quality data. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark and three real-world manipulation tasks show that RedFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art offline RL baselines, improving the real-world success rate from 56.7% to 74.7%. It also matches strong on-policy methods (PPO, GRPO, and DDPO) while requiring roughly an order of magnitude fewer training samples.

</details>


### [302] [DexDirect: Direct Kinesthetic Arm Guidance for Efficient Dexterous Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27784)
*Beom Jun Kim,Shiu-Jen Wang,Jonathan Liu,Alvin Zhu,Quanyou Wang,Hanzhang Fang,Feng Xu,Mingzhang Zhu,Yuchen Cui,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDirect是一种结合直接动觉臂引导和基于视觉的手部重定向的去手操作演示收集接口，相比纯视觉和姿态追踪基线，显著提高了成功演示的数量并降低了认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去手操作演示收集方法在保真度和成本之间存在权衡：高保真接口昂贵且复杂，而低成本替代方案控制精度低且认知负荷高。

Method: 操作者通过手柄直接拖动6-DoF重力补偿机器人臂，同时使用单个网络摄像头将操作者的另一只手重定向到16关节13-DoF的机器人手上。

Result: 用户研究显示，与纯视觉（AnyTeleop）和姿态追踪（TeleDex）基线相比，DexDirect收集的成功演示数量分别提高了17.2倍和3.2倍；改编的NASA-TLX显示DexDirect大幅降低了脑力需求、努力和挫败感，尽管身体需求有所增加；基于DexDirect演示训练的扩散策略在立方体抓取放置任务上达到90%的成功率。

Conclusion: 直接动觉臂引导结合视觉手部重定向提供了一种高效、低设置成本且可扩展的去手操作演示收集接口。

Abstract: Scalable collection of dexterous manipulation demonstrations remains a major bottleneck for robot learning. High-fidelity interfaces often require costly hardware and extensive setup, while low-setup, low cost alternatives tend to provide less precise control and impose greater cognitive workload on operators. We present DexDirect, a direct kinesthetic arm guidance for efficient dexterous demonstration collection. The operator drags a 6-DoF gravity-compensated robot arm directly by a handle, while a single webcam retargets operator's other hand onto a 16 joints 13-DoF dexterous robot hand. User studies suggest DexDirect collects 17.2x and 3.2x more successful demonstrations compared to purely vision (AnyTeleop) and pose-tracking (TeleDex) baselines. An adapted NASA-TLX shows DexDirect greatly reduces mental demand, effort, and frustration, despite raising physical demand. A diffusion policy trained on DexDirect demonstrations reaches a 90% success rate on a cube pick-and-place task. These results suggest that direct kinesthetic arm guidance combined with vision-based hand retargeting provides an efficient low-setup and scalable interface for collecting dexterous manipulation demonstrations

</details>


### [303] [RoboBRIDGE: A Modular Framework for Bridging Policies to Robust Real-World Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27881)
*Sihyung Yoon,Minjong Yoo,Sanghyun Ahn,Seojeong Choi,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboBRIDGE是一个模块化框架，通过协调监控、感知、规划、控制和机器人接口五个模块，将预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型转化为可靠的机器人智能体，解决故障恢复、长期执行不一致和分布偏移等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中虽有效，但直接部署时缺乏故障恢复机制、长时序执行不稳定且对观测、任务或本体变化敏感。现有方法仅单独解决这些问题，需要通用框架系统性地将VLA转化为机器人智能体。

Method: RoboBRIDGE包含五个协调模块：Monitor用于快速故障检测和分层恢复；Planner在环境偏离计划时触发重新规划；Perceptor异步更新场景理解；Controller通过技能微调将操作分解为领域不变原语并采用LoRA适配器降低领域偏移；Robot Interface提供交互接口。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa和真实世界案例中，覆盖多种机器人平台和VLA骨干，RoboBRIDGE在性能上持续优于独立策略和先前的增强VLA部署。

Conclusion: 可靠的机器人智能体不仅来自扩展动作预测器，更依赖于围绕其构建的结构化编排。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have attracted growing interest as a scalable approach to robotic manipulation. While these models are effective action predictors, deploying them as robotic agents exposes critical gaps: no mechanism for failure recovery, inconsistent execution over long horizons, and limited robustness to shifts in observations, tasks, or embodiments. Existing solutions address these limitations individually through model retraining or environment-specific modules, yet what is needed is a general framework that systematically transforms a pretrained VLA into a robotic agent. We present RoboBRIDGE, a modular framework that provides an orchestration layer over five coordinated modules, namely Monitor, Perceptor, Planner, Controller, and Robot Interface, to compose robust robotic agents from off-the-shelf components, including pretrained VLAs. The Monitor pairs rapid failure detection with hierarchical recovery to correct errors before they cascade. When the environment diverges from the current plan, the Planner triggers replanning while the Perceptor updates scene understanding asynchronously, avoiding execution stalls. Within the Controller, primitive skill fine-tuning factors manipulation into domain-invariant primitives with dedicated LoRA adapters, reducing sensitivity to domain shifts when a VLA is used. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world case studies spanning multiple robot platforms and VLA backbones, RoboBRIDGE consistently outperforms both standalone policies and prior augmented VLA deployments. These results suggest that reliable robotic agency does not arise from scaling action predictors alone, but from structured orchestration around them.

</details>


### [304] [Static In, Dynamic Out: Counterfactual Action Augmentation for Moving Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27890)
*Woo Chul Shin,Zhenyang Chen,Alfred Cueva,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Yingyan Celine Lin,Benjamin Joffe,Shreyas Kousik,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SIDO方法，通过反事实动作增强，使仅基于静态物体演示训练的视觉运动策略能够适应测试时的物体运动，在模拟和真实任务中均提升了动态物体操作的成功率，同时保持静态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略在目标物体静止时表现良好，但实际场景中物体常动态变化（如传送带漂移、风中摆动），导致策略失效，需要一种方法让静态训练的策略适应动态物体。

Method: SIDO将动态操作分解为预测物体未来位置和到达该位置两个子问题，通过将物体替换到反事实未来位置并调整演示动作以保持手-物相对位姿，生成目标条件策略。部署时使用物体位姿预测器提供未来位置。

Result: 在三个模拟任务（Mug、Square、Stack）和五种物体运动模式下，以及两个真实任务（Gantry、Peachtree）中，SIDO提升了动态物体操作成功率，同时不损害静态物体性能。

Conclusion: SIDO有效地弥合了静态训练与动态测试之间的差距，为实际环境中的鲁棒操作提供了新思路，且无需额外的动态演示数据。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies have advanced on manipulation tasks where the target object stays static during execution, but real deployments break this assumption: parts drift on conveyors and fruits sway in the wind. We introduce Static In, Dynamic Out (SIDO), a counterfactual action augmentation that enables a policy trained only on static object demonstrations to adapt to unseen object motion at test time. Our key idea is to factorize moving object manipulation into two sub-problems: predicting where the object will be, and reaching that predicted pose. SIDO displaces the object to a counterfactual future position and morphs the demonstrated action chunk to preserve the hand-object relative pose, yielding a goal-conditioned policy. At deployment an object pose predictor supplies the future position. Across three simulated tasks (Mug, Square, Stack) under five object motion patterns and two real-world tasks (Gantry, Peachtree), SIDO improves moving object success over the baselines while preserving static object performance. Project website: https://sido-staticindynamicout.github.io/.

</details>


### [305] [Learning Social Robot Navigation By Sensing Human Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27922)
*Alberto Vaglio,Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Tommaso Van Der Meer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CALF，一种端到端神经网络架构，直接处理2D LiDAR中的腿部运动以生成安全导航命令，并在轻量级模拟器LegNav中训练，实现零样本部署到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航方法将行人简化为圆形，忽视了LiDAR主要捕获腿部运动的事实，导致社交合规性不足。

Method: 结合卷积层、注意力和MLP的CALF架构，使用深度强化学习在自定义2D模拟器LegNav中训练，该模拟器集成了2D LiDAR射线追踪和新颖的行人步态模型。

Result: 在模拟和真实TurtleBot 4实验中，CALF相比基线的导航性能和社会合规性更优，训练时间不到一小时。

Conclusion: 直接利用腿部特征可显著提升机器人导航的安全性和社交合规性，且轻量级模拟器支持高效训练和实际部署。

Abstract: Robots navigating among pedestrians typically sense their surroundings with a 2D LiDAR mounted close to the ground. At that height, the sensor mostly sees moving legs rather than whole people, yet most learning-based navigation methods still treat pedestrians as simple shapes like circles. This paper addresses that gap with CALF (Convolutional Attention for Leg Features), an end-to-end neural architecture that combines convolutional layers, attention, and MLP to interpret leg motion directly from LiDAR scans and produce safe navigation commands. The CALF policy is trained using deep reinforcement learning algorithms within LegNav, a custom lightweight 2D simulator that combines 2D LiDAR ray tracing with a novel pedestrian gait model. The resulting policy is compared against classical and learning-based baselines in terms of navigation performance and social compliance. The approach is validated through real-world experiments via zero-shot deployment on a TurtleBot 4, yielding smooth and socially compliant trajectories. Written in JAX, the LegNav simulator enables the training of a deployment-ready CALF policy in under an hour on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [306] [RaDiVe: Robust 4D Radar Odometry with Distance-Bounded NDT and Velocity-Discrepancy Point Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28045)
*Sangwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,Chiyun Noh,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RaDiVe，一种4D雷达里程计框架，通过距离受限的NDT、速度差异点不确定性模型和SDF表面点提取，显著提升配准精度和鲁棒性，在公共数据集上平均降低平移ATE 44.4%、旋转ATE 21.3%，并保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达在恶劣天气下感知鲁棒，但其点云稀疏、噪声大、位置精度有限，导致基于配准的里程计面临挑战，现有方法精度和鲁棒性不足。

Method: 采用距离受限的NDT（限制近距体素对搜索）提高优化稳定性；提出速度差异点不确定性模型（利用多普勒径向速度与预测速度的差异加权点）缓解测量模糊；并利用隐式神经映射的SDF提取表面点，构建几何一致且去噪的局部子图。

Result: 在多个公共数据集上，RaDiVe相比现有4D雷达里程计基线，平均平移ATE降低44.4%，旋转ATE降低21.3%，且保持实时性能。

Conclusion: RaDiVe有效提升了4D雷达点云配准的准确性和鲁棒性，适用于恶劣天气下的自主导航，代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D radar enable robust perception in adverse weather; however, the inherent sparsity, noise, and limited positional precision of radar point clouds pose significant challenges for registration-based odometry. In this letter, we propose RaDiVe, a 4D radar odometry framework designed to improve the accuracy and robustness of radar point-cloud registration. We introduce a distance-bounded Normal Distributions Transform (NDT), which improves optimization stability and computational efficiency by restricting the correspondence search to near-distance voxel pairs. To mitigate measurement ambiguity, we propose a velocity-discrepancy point uncertainty model that weights each input 4D radar point according to the discrepancy between its measured Doppler radial velocity and the radial velocity predicted from the estimated ego-velocity. Furthermore, we incorporate Signed Distance Function (SDF)-based surface point extraction via implicit neural mapping to construct a geometrically consistent and noise-filtered local submap. Evaluations across multiple public datasets demonstrate that RaDiVe outperforms existing 4D radar odometry baselines by 44.4% in translational Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and 21.3% in rotational ATE on average, while maintaining real-time performance. The source code will be made publicly available to the robotics community: https://github.com/to-be-open-sourced.

</details>


### [307] [UniCross: Unified Cross-Skill Dexterous Manipulation Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28198)
*Hui Zhang,Julian Ferchow,Jie Song,Mirko Meboldt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，将抓取、重定位、手内旋转和手内平移四种灵巧操作技能建模为单一任务公式，支持跨技能策略学习、泛化、鲁棒性和长期组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将灵巧操作技能分别建模，具有不同的动作约束、目标和专用手部形态，破坏了长期组合所需的兼容性和连续性。

Method: 统一四种技能的状态、动作空间和目标结构，采用单一公式，通过蒸馏学习跨技能策略，并支持不同手部形态的迁移。

Result: 单一跨技能策略在每种技能上表现优异，泛化到未见对象，对干扰鲁棒，并能无缝组合成长期操作，且在手部形态间有效迁移。

Conclusion: 不同灵巧操作技能可视为共享任务公式的实例，揭示了它们之间的内在一致性。

Abstract: Many dexterous manipulation tasks require the object to remain securely held throughout the interaction. From the perspective of hand-object relational motion, such manipulation comprises four canonical skills: grasping, relocation, in-hand rotation, and in-hand translation. Human hands flexibly compose these skills to accomplish complex tasks. Existing approaches, however, model these skills separately with skill-specific action constraints, objectives, or even dedicated hand morphologies, which breaks the compatibility and continuity required for long-horizon composition. In this work, we present a unified framework that models all four skills in a single formulation that shares the same state and action spaces and a common objective structure. This formulation enables straightforward distillation of a single cross-skill policy that performs strongly on every skill, generalizes to unseen objects, stays robust to disturbances, and chains skills seamlessly into long-horizon manipulation. The framework also transfers effectively across different hand morphologies. Overall, our results suggest that different dexterous manipulation skills can be viewed as instantiations of a shared task formulation, revealing the intrinsic consistency across different behaviors.

</details>


### [308] [When Robots Exchange Meaning: A Demo of Goal-Oriented Semantic Communications for Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28256)
*Peizheng Li,Xinyi Lin,Sajida Gufran,Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个机器人-边缘语义通信测试平台，将视觉压缩、语义映射和任务交互集成到6G协作机器人场景中，实现42.67倍压缩率和3D语义地图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信质量指标（如分组投递率）难以反映协作机器人任务执行的实际效果，需要将通信与任务语义结合，推动面向任务的6G通信研究。

Method: 采用VQ-VAE编码器在机器人端进行视觉压缩（ONNX Runtime），边缘端用PyTorch解码器重建图像，结合RTAB-Map生成语义3D地图，并通过浏览器仪表板进行任务交互。

Result: 320×240图像压缩为80×60 token网格（5400字节），实现42.67倍压缩；成功演示从视觉传输到对象级地图交互的完整流程。

Conclusion: 该测试平台为语义通信与具身智能/物理AI在6G网络中的交叉研究提供了实践基础，支持未来任务感知网络设计。

Abstract: Collaborative robotics is a representative task-oriented 6G use-case, where communication quality should be reflected in mission execution, environment understanding, and closed-loop operation rather than packet delivery alone. This demo paper presents a robot-edge semantic communication (SemCom) testbed integrating robot-side visual compression, edge-side semantic mapping, and dashboard-based mission interaction. A mobile robot equipped with RGB-D sensing and LiDAR runs ROS 2, while a Jetson Orin edge node performs reconstruction, RTAB-Map mapping, semantic object handling, and browserbased visualization. As an initial proof of concept, RGB frames are encoded on the robot into VQ-VAE tokens using an ONNX Runtime encoder and reconstructed on the edge using a PyTorch decoder. A 320 X 240 image is represented by an 80 X 60 token grid with a packed payload of 5400 bytes, corresponding to a 42.67X reduction relative to model-input RGB bytes. The reconstructed visual stream is further associated with depth, pose, and 3D mapping information to generate a semantic map for downstream robotic applications. The demo exposes the full path from semantic visual transport to object-level map interaction, and provides a practical platform for future task-aware 6G networking studies at the intersection of SemCom, embodied AI, and physical AI-enabled robotics. A video of the demo is available at https://tinyurl.com/Tos09

</details>


### [309] [SemAnCorr: Semantic Anchored Correspondence for Zero-Shot Manipulation Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28382)
*Xiaoxiang Dong,William Baron,Hongyi Chen,Uksang Yoo,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SemAnCorr，一种无需训练的框架，通过联合位姿-对应优化选择语义一致的锚定区域，并利用函数映射在物体表面传播约束，从而建立语义一致且几何连贯的稠密对应，实现跨几何形状的物体操作技能迁移。在PartNet-Mobility基准上达到90.8%的语义准确率，并在真实机器人上显著提升零样本技能迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨不同几何形状但功能相同的物体迁移操作技能是机器人学习中的基本挑战。现有基于密集视觉描述符和3D特征场的方法，其最近邻特征匹配常产生空间不连贯的对应，无法恢复所需的局部几何框架，导致技能迁移不可靠。

Method: SemAnCorr：一种无需训练的框架，通过联合位姿-对应优化选择语义一致的锚定区域，并利用函数映射在物体表面传播约束，从而获得语义一致且几何连贯的稠密对应。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility基准上达到90.8%的语义准确率，几何连贯性优于现有最先进方法；在真实机器人实验中，仅需一次示范即可显著提高零样本技能迁移的可靠性。

Conclusion: SemAnCorr通过结合语义一致性和几何连贯性，有效提升了跨几何形状的操作技能迁移性能，且无需额外训练，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Transferring manipulation skills across object instances that share functionality but differ in geometry remains a fundamental challenge in robot learning. While recent correspondence methods leverage dense visual descriptors and 3D feature fields, nearest-neighbor feature matching often produces spatially incoherent correspondences that fail to recover the local geometric frames required for reliable skill transfer. We introduce SemAnCorr, a training-free framework that establishes dense correspondence by selecting semantically consistent anchor regions through joint pose-correspondence optimization and propagating these constraints over the object surface using functional maps. The resulting correspondences preserve both semantic consistency and geometric coherence, enabling object-centric manipulation skills to transfer across geometrically diverse instances. We evaluate SemAnCorr on a dense correspondence benchmark built on PartNet-Mobility, achieving 90.8% semantic accuracy in our benchmark evaluation while improving geometric coherence over recent state-of-the-art baselines. Finally, we show that these improvements translate directly into real-world manipulation performance: using a single demonstration, SemAnCorr enables substantially more reliable zero-shot manipulation skill transfer to previously unseen objects than existing correspondence methods. Videos and additional visualizations are available at [https://semancorr.github.io](https://semancorr.github.io) .

</details>


### [310] [TacWAM: Anchor-Guided World Action Model with Mechanics-Aware Tactile Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28391)
*Lei Jin,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacWAM，一种结合触觉未来预测与动作生成的机械感知世界模型，通过三模态编码、时间上下文和注意力分离，在接触丰富操作任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要依赖视觉预测，缺乏对力、变形、剪切和滑移等接触物理信息的监督，导致接触丰富操作学习受限。

Method: 采用SAF触觉编码器融合触觉外观、力场和变形流，并通过双边力/力矩重建保留全局接触信息；引入触觉历史编码器提供时间上下文；使用AGT注意力分离视觉/触觉锚点、未来预测和动作令牌，防止未来触觉信息被动作分支直接利用。

Result: 在四个真实接触丰富操作任务上，TacWAM平均成功率达75.0%，比最强基线高37.5个百分点；消融实验显示移除触觉历史或放松未来预测约束会一致降低性能。

Conclusion: 结合信息丰富的触觉表示和部署一致的约束，未来触觉监督能有效提升接触感知的动作学习。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine future-state prediction with robot action generation, but existing approaches largely rely on visual futures. Visual prediction captures scene structure and object motion, yet provides limited supervision for force, deformation, shear, and slip during contact-rich manipulation. This creates two design requirements: tactile futures should carry meaningful physical information, and they should not become privileged cues for action generation. We present TacWAM, a mechanics-aware tactile WAM that addresses this challenge in three steps. First, a Spatially Aligned Fusion (SAF) Tactile Encoder maps tactile appearance, dense force fields, and deformation flow into a shared latent prediction space, with bilateral force and torque reconstruction preserving global contact information. Second, a tactile history encoder provides temporal context so future tactile prediction reflects how force and deformation change beyond the current tactile observation. Third, Anchor-Guided Tri-Modal (AGT) Attention separates current visual and tactile anchors, future prediction tokens, and action tokens, allowing future tactile states to supervise training without being directly read by the action branch. We evaluate TacWAM on four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks covering fragile grasping, sustained surface contact, and dynamic in-hand manipulation. TacWAM achieves an average success rate of 75.0%, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 37.5 percentage points. Staged ablations show consistent degradation when tactile history is removed and access to future prediction targets is relaxed. These results indicate that future tactile supervision can improve contact-aware action learning when combined with informative tactile representations and deployment-consistent information constraints.

</details>


### [311] [FasTac: A Curved Multispectral Vision-Based Tactile Sensor for High-Speed High-Precision 3D Shape and Force Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28416)
*Xiaofan Lu,Kaiji Huang,Jiahui Chen,Yuankai Lin,Hua Yang,Zhouping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FasTac，一种结合多光谱光度立体、动态卷积力估计和FPGA硬件加速的曲面视觉触觉传感器，实现了高精度三维重建、三轴力估计和高速处理，实验验证了其在精细几何感知、稳定力反馈和动态接触感知方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲面指尖在灵巧操作中需要解析精细接触几何、区分法向和切向载荷并捕捉瞬态信号，但现有曲面视觉触觉传感器难以在紧凑形态下兼顾三维重建精度、三轴力估计和高速处理。

Method: 采用单图像传感器同步多光谱成像获得空间对齐观测，用于鲁棒表面法线估计；通过边界先验快速泊松重建获取深度；提出HyperForce使用位置感知动态卷积建模曲面弹性体的空间非均匀机械响应以估计三轴力；完整图像到法向力流水线部署于FPGA。

Result: 近红外照明和边界先验使深度平均绝对误差从0.2730mm降至0.0415mm；HyperForce法向和剪切力归一化平均绝对误差分别为2.74%和2.39%；FPGA部署将处理延迟从GPU上的3.26ms缩短至1.09ms。

Conclusion: FasTac通过技术集成和硬件加速，在紧凑曲面传感器中实现了高精度三维重建、准确三轴力估计和高速动态响应，适用于复杂灵巧操作任务。

Abstract: Curved tactile fingertips for dexterous manipulation must resolve fine contact geometry, distinguish normal and tangential loads, and capture transient signals. Existing curved vision-based tactile sensors struggle to combine accurate 3D reconstruction, three-axis force estimation, and high-speed processing in a compact form. This article presents FasTac, a curved vision-based tactile sensor integrating multispectral photometric stereo, dynamic-convolution force estimation, and hardware acceleration on a field-programmable gate array (FPGA). Single-image-sensor simultaneous multispectral imaging provides spatially aligned observations for robust surface normal estimation, followed by boundary-prior fast Poisson depth reconstruction. HyperForce uses position-aware dynamic convolution to model the spatially nonuniform mechanical response of curved elastomers and estimate three-axis forces. The complete image-to-normal-force pipeline is deployed on an FPGA. Experiments show that near-infrared (NIR) illumination and the boundary prior decrease depth mean absolute error (MAE) from 0.2730 mm to 0.0415 mm; HyperForce achieves normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) values of 2.74% and 2.39% for normal and shear forces, respectively; and FPGA deployment shortens processing latency from 3.26 ms on the GPU to 1.09 ms. Multi-object reconstruction, feedback grasping, and vibration measurement validate fine geometric perception, stable force feedback, and dynamic contact sensing.

</details>


### [312] [One Future, Every Robot: Label-Efficient Collective-State Prediction with Decentralized JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28443)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Garvilov,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出集体状态JEPA（CS-JEPA），一种去中心化共享状态预测架构，使每个机器人仅通过局部观测和带宽受限消息预测共同未来状态，并在拓扑和规模变化下显著优于基线，且对规划有潜在价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人集群预测依赖全局信息或中心化处理，但实际中机器人只能获得局部观测和有限带宽消息，且缺乏标签。因此需要一种无监督、可扩展的预测方法，使所有机器人能一致地预测未来集体状态。

Method: CS-JEPA是一种循环联合嵌入预测架构，利用16帧局部历史和每条有向边的64浮点循环消息，避免全局池化、目标编码器、事件时钟或记录的未来动作。预训练无下游集体标签，冻结表示后用岭探针评估。

Result: 在最多108个机器人的多种拓扑（分布内、环、互kNN、未见规模）上，CS-JEPA在预测误差和机器人间一致性的标签预算AUC上优于具有额外9607个参数的原始未来重建基线（5/5外种子）。在密封八种子测试中，CS-JEPA将分支价值MSE降低45.5%，并提升候选分数相关性（8/8种子，包括N=32）。

Conclusion: 共同未来JEPA目标是一种标签高效的原始单元，适用于拓扑和规模变化下的去中心化集群预测，并支持规划相关的价值估计。

Abstract: Can every robot in a swarm predict the same future collective state from only local observations and bandwidth-limited messages? We formulate this as decentralized shared-state prediction and introduce Collective-State JEPA (CS-JEPA), a recurrent joint-embedding predictive architecture whose output at every robot represents one common future token field. At deployment, each robot uses a 16-frame local history and one 64-float recurrent message per directed edge; there is no global pooling, target encoder, episode clock, or recorded future action. After pretraining without downstream collective labels, frozen representations are evaluated with ridge probes fitted on 6, 12, or 24 globally labeled episodes. Against raw-future reconstruction with the same receiver anchor and deployment capacity but 9,607 additional training-only parameters, a prospectively registered five-seed follow-up improves prediction-error and inter-robot-agreement label-budget AUC on in-distribution, ring, mutual-kNN, and unseen-size families up to 108 robots. Every effect favors CS-JEPA in 5/5 outer seeds. In a separate sealed eight-seed follow-up, matched action-conditioned predictors receive each candidate four-step plan before producing receiver-local predictive representations. CS-JEPA reduces branch-value MSE by 45.5% and improves within-context candidate-score Pearson correlation by 0.1291, with both effects favorable in 8/8 seeds, including at unseen N=32. These results support common-future JEPA targets as a label-efficient primitive for decentralized swarm prediction under topology and size shift, with additional evidence of planning-relevant value estimation.

</details>


### [313] [Machines that know they are aging: a framework for hardware-aware autonomous intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28451)
*Cheng Siong Chin,Jianhua Zhang,Mohan Venkateshkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出老化感知自主智能（AAAI）框架，通过将硬件健康状态融入推理、规划和任务执行，解决自主系统因硬件老化导致的性能下降和任务失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统硬件会老化，而AI通常假设硬件条件不变，导致实际能力与假设不匹配，引发任务失败。现有方法缺乏对硬件老化的主动感知和适应。

Method: AAAI框架包含三大支柱：硬件自感知（基于物理失效模型估计电源、传感、内存和计算子系统的健康）、自适应推理（根据剩余硬件能力调整推理复杂度、规划周期和任务优先级）、生存中心智能（通过性能优化、资源节约和优雅降级分配剩余运行寿命）。

Result: AAAI通过集成预测、生命周期管理和硬件感知计算，提高系统韧性、延长运行寿命，并支持更安全、更优雅的任务完成。

Conclusion: AAAI对运行在不可达或安全关键环境中的自主系统（如太空任务、海洋机器人和植入式医疗设备）至关重要，使机器能识别并响应自身老化，提升可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous systems inevitably age, yet their artificial intelligence typically assumes hardware remains in its original condition. Batteries degrade, sensors drift, processors accumulate timing errors, and memory reliability declines, creating a growing mismatch between assumed and actual capability. This can lead to agnostic collapse, where mission failure arises from accumulated hardware degradation rather than a single component fault. We propose Aging-Aware Autonomous Intelligence (AAAI), a framework that integrates hardware health directly into reasoning, planning, and mission execution. AAAI is built on three pillars: hardware self-awareness, which continuously estimates the health of power, sensing, memory, and computation subsystems using physics-of-failure models; self-adaptive reasoning, which adjusts inference complexity, planning horizon, and task priorities according to remaining hardware capability; and survival-centric intelligence, which allocates remaining operational life across mission objectives through performance optimization, resource conservation, and graceful degradation. Rather than introducing new hardware, AAAI unifies prognostics, lifecycle management, and hardware-aware computing into a closed-loop cognitive architecture. We argue that such integration is essential for autonomous systems operating in inaccessible or safety-critical environments, including space missions, marine robotics, and implantable medical devices. By enabling machines to recognize and respond to their own aging, AAAI improves resilience, extends operational lifetime, and supports safer, more graceful mission completion.

</details>


### [314] [TEA-AgriVLN: Traversability Estimation Alarm for Agricultural Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28474)
*Xiaobei Zhao,Xingqi Lyu,Xin Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TEA模块，通过估计图像可穿越性并提醒决策者重新思考，提升农业场景VLN-CE的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业场景中区域可穿越性模糊（如未成熟玉米地对机器狗可穿越但对人不可），导致导航决策困难。

Method: 在AgriVLN骨干中集成TEA模块，估计相机图像的可穿越性，当预测动作与可穿越性地图不一致时触发重新思考。

Result: 在A2A基准上，成功率从0.47提升至0.54，导航误差从2.91米降至2.70米，达到当前最优。

Conclusion: TEA-AgriVLN在农业VLN-CE中有效提升导航性能，但存在局限，需进一步研究不同地面类别和场景。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to follow a natural language instruction, predicting a sequence of low-level actions to navigate a robot from a starting point to a target location. The A2A benchmark and the AgriVLN method pioneeringly extended VLN-CE from indoor scenes to agricultural scenes, while we observed a challenging distinction: In indoor scenes, whether a zone is traversable tends to be clear to classify, such as wood floors are traversable but concrete walls are not. In agricultural scenes, however, this issue tends to be ambiguous, such as an unripe cornfield might be traversable for a robotic dog but might be non-traversable for a human. To address this issue, we propose the TEA module, which estimates the traversability of the camera image, then alarm the decision-maker for rethinking when the predicted action does not align with the traversability map. We integrate it into the AgriVLN backbone to build our TEA-AgriVLN method. When evaluated on A2A, it improves Success Rate (SR) from 0.47 to 0.54 and Navigation Error (NE) from 2.91 m to 2.70 m, showing the state-of-the-art performance in the agricultural VLN-CE domain. We further implement the ablation studies and the case study, discussing the effectiveness and limitations of TEA on different ground categories and scene classes. Code: https://github.com/AlexTraveling/TEA-AgriVLN.

</details>


### [315] [X-NavDP: Generalizing Navigation Diffusion Policy to Novel Behavior and Embodiments with Group Q-score Reweighted Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28560)
*Tianyu Yang,Yiming Zeng,Wenzhe Cai,Yuqiang Yang,Jiaqi Peng,Hui Cheng,Jiangmiao Pang,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GQRM框架，通过自引导探索和组Q值归一化机制，对预训练的导航扩散策略进行RL后训练，提升跨 embodiment 的视觉导航性能，成功率显著提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练导航扩散策略依赖大量专家数据，但数据由单一机器人的全知规划器生成，限制了策略对不同 embodiment 和挑战场景的泛化。RL后训练可缓解此问题，但现有方法因扩散策略似然不可解导致梯度不稳定和探索低效，改进有限。

Method: 提出GQRM框架，包括两个互补设计：(i) 自引导探索策略，通过行为扰动保留预训练先验；(ii) 组Q值归一化机制，计算每轨迹各状态的Q值，用于高效重加权分数匹配。训练采用分布式在线RL，跨异构 embodiment 进行。

Result: 微调后的策略X-NavDP在仿真中整体成功率从61.20%提升至84.28%，在真实世界困难场景中从10%提升至65%，达到跨 embodiment 视觉导航的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GQRM框架有效提升了扩散策略的RL后训练效率和性能，显著增强了导航策略在多样 embodiment 和复杂场景下的泛化能力，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Pretraining navigation diffusion policies rely on large-scale expert demonstrations. These data are typically generated by a fully-informed oracle planner suited to a single nominal robot. This limits the policy's generalization to diverse embodiments and challenging scenarios (e.g., escaping dead ends or detouring long obstacles) that demand diverse local reactive behaviors with only onboard local observations. Post-training the policy with reinforcement learning (RL) offers a principled remedy. However, previous RL for diffusion approaches lead to only marginal improvements. This is because the intractable likelihood of diffusion policies renders policy gradients unstable in addition to inefficient policy exploration. To address these challenges, we propose a data-efficient diffusion RL post-training framework - GQRM (Group Q-score Reweighted Matching). Our framework introduces two complementary designs: (i) a self-bootstrapped exploration strategy with behavior perturbation that preserves the pretrained policy prior, and (ii) a group Q-score normalization mechanism that computes per-trajectory values on each state for efficient reweighted score matching. By conducting distributed online RL training across heterogeneous embodiments, the resulting fine-tuned policy, X-NavDP, achieves state-of-the-art cross-embodiment visual navigation performance, improving the overall success rate from 61.20% to 84.28% in simulation and 10% to 65% in real-world hard cases. The code and model are publicly available at https://yty-sky.github.io/x-navdp-project-page.

</details>


### [316] [FA-RDP: A Frequency-Adaptive Reactive Diffusion Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28596)
*Lifeng Zhuo,Wendi Chen,Han Xue,Shirun Tang,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FA-RDP，一种频率自适应的反应式扩散策略，通过多频视觉-力Transformer和学习的多模态指示器，在接触前使用低频多步采样保持动作多模态，接触后切换高频单步采样提升反应性，并引入流形一致性蒸馏（MCD）增强动作可行性，在三个接触丰富的操作任务中取得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散策略在接触丰富的操作中面临固定推理频率与采样步数的权衡：低频多步采样保留接触前的多模态但反应慢，高频采样反应快但可能崩溃多模态，因此需要频率自适应机制。

Method: 提出FA-RDP，包含共享多频视觉-力Transformer预测高低频动作块；学习多模态指示器动态选择低频多步采样（接触前）或高频单步采样（接触后）；引入流形一致性蒸馏（MCD），将扩散网络重参数化以预测机器人动作流形上的动作，同时保留DDPM残差监督。

Result: 在三个接触丰富的操作任务中，FA-RDP达到最高成功率，同时保留了接触前的多样轨迹模式。

Conclusion: FA-RDP通过频率自适应和流形蒸馏有效解决了接触操作中的多模态保持与反应性权衡，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In contact-rich manipulation, action multimodality and reactivity dominate different stages of a single episode. Before contact, multiple trajectories might be equally valid, making it important to preserve diverse action modes. After contact, geometric constraints and force limits narrow the solution space, while successful execution demands rapid responses to force feedback. However, standard diffusion policies use a fixed inference frequency and sampling steps throughout the episode, forcing a fundamental compromise: low-frequency, multi-step sampling better preserves pre-contact multimodality but responds slowly to force feedback, whereas high-frequency sampling improves reactivity but tends to collapse distinct pre-contact modes. To resolve this tradeoff, we present FA-RDP, a frequency-adaptive reactive diffusion policy. A shared multi-frequency visual-force Transformer predicts action chunks at both low and high frequencies, while a learned multimodality indicator dynamically selects multi-step low-frequency sampling before contact and one-step high-frequency sampling as action ambiguity decreases. We further introduce Manifold Consistency Distillation (MCD), which reparameterizes the diffusion network to predict actions on the robot action manifold while retaining DDPM-based residual supervision. Experiments on three contact-rich manipulation tasks show that FA-RDP achieves the highest success rate while preserving diverse pre-contact trajectory modes. Code and videos are available at https://fa-rdp.github.io.

</details>


### [317] [PAC-MAN: Perception-Aware CBF-RL for Whole-Body Safety in Humanoid Dodgeball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28623)
*Lizhi Yang,Junheng Li,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 我们提出了PAC-MAN，一个感知感知的CBF-RL框架，将控制屏障安全性与部署现实的机载感知结合，用于全身人形躲避球。策略仅通过头戴相机的分割掩膜深度感知球，训练时的CBF指导表示所有身体连杆的间隙，对抗运动先验正则化逃避反射。在受控的任意连杆接触基准上，单次投掷和部署循环中，策略性能接近特权状态预言机。发现屏障结构依赖感知可观测性：联合CBF在准确球状态时性能最佳，在固定相机观察下作为训练指导时性能下降，但通过球跟踪云台或特权运行时滤波器可恢复。最终在Unitree G1上零样本部署轻量级Link-CBF策略，容忍不完美感知，成功躲避95%的投掷，并使用语义分割躲避不同球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBF-RL方法通常在训练中假设理想化感知，且多用于固定基座或简单动力学，缺乏对全身人形机器人在感知受限条件下（如仅机载相机）的躲避任务研究。因此，需要一种耦合感知感知的CBF-RL框架，以实现在真实世界部署中可靠的动态躲避行为。

Method: 提出PAC-MAN框架，包含：1) 训练中使用CBF指导表示身体连杆的间隙，提供安全屏障；2) 部署时使用头戴相机的分割掩膜深度作为观测，避免对精确球状态的依赖；3) 引入对抗运动先验正则化逃避行为；4) 在仿真中对比不同感知配置（固定相机、云台、特权滤波器）和CBF类型（Joint-CBF vs Link-CBF）；5) 在Unitree G1上零样本部署Link-CBF策略。

Result: 在仿真基准上，PAC-MAN策略表现接近特权状态预言机，仅需固定机载相机即可规避；Joint-CBF在准确球状态时最佳，但固定相机下性能下降，云台或滤波器可恢复；Link-CBF在真实世界实验中成功躲避95%的投掷，并可由语义分割区分不同球。

Conclusion: 感知感知的CBF-RL框架能够在现实硬件上实现有效的全身动态躲避，且轻量级Link-CBF策略对不完美感知具有鲁棒性，适合部署。

Abstract: We present PAC-MAN, a perception-aware CBF-RL framework that couples control-barrier safety with deployment-realistic onboard sensing for whole-body humanoid dodgeball. The deployed policy sees the ball only as segmentation-masked depth from a head-mounted camera, while training-time CBF guidance represents clearance to every body link, and an adversarial motion prior regularizes the resulting evasive reflexes. We evaluate on a controlled any-link contact benchmark with seeded throws in two regimes: single throws and a deployment loop in which the robot walks back to its station and recovers between throws. On this benchmark, the policy comes within a few points of a privileged state oracle: a fixed onboard camera alone is adequate for evasion. We find that usable barrier structure depends on perceptual observability: Joint-CBF gives the best performance with accurate ball states, degrades under fixed-camera observations when used only as training guidance, and recovers with a ball-tracking gimbal or privileged runtime filter. We therefore deploy a lightweight Link-CBF policy zero-shot on the Unitree G1 in the real world, where it tolerates imperfect perception, succeeds on 95% of throws, and uses semantic segmentation to dodge different balls.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [318] [Structural Averaged Controllability for Linear Ensemble Systems: Multi-input Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27706)
*Amirreza Neshaei Moghaddam,Xudong Chen,Bahman Gharesifard*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了多输入线性系综系统的结构平均可控性，给出了完整刻画：除可达性外，核心存在行饱和匹配是充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单输入情形的结构平均可控性已被完全刻画，但更一般的多输入情形此前尚未解决。

Method: 通过引入系统的核心（一个与单输入情形相关的无圈子图），并证明其行饱和匹配的存在性，结合可达性条件，给出充要条件。

Result: 证明了多输入线性系综系统结构平均可控的充要条件为：系统满足可达性且核心存在行饱和匹配。

Conclusion: 首次完整刻画了多输入线性系综系统的结构平均可控性，推广了单输入结果，并揭示了核心结构在其中的中心作用。

Abstract: We study structural averaged controllability for multi-input linear ensemble systems. In this problem, one asks whether a sparsity pattern admits a linear ensemble system that is averaged controllable. The single-input case has been characterized completely, while the general multi-input case has remained open. In this work, we give a complete characterization for the general multi-input case. In particular, we prove that in addition to accessibility, a necessary and sufficient condition for structural averaged controllability of ensemble systems is existence of a row-saturating matching for its \emph{core}, an acyclic subgraph associated with the system which plays a central role in the result for the single-input case.

</details>


### [319] [Estimated-State Adaptive Sliding Mode Control and Disturbance Observation Using Second-Order Surfaces for Spacecraft Formation Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27524)
*Jaein Lee,Hancheol Cho,Tiago Roux Oliveira*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段相对轨道控制框架，结合解析能量最优转移与自适应滑模跟踪，实现航天器编队飞行的高精度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对航天器编队飞行中相对轨道转移与保持问题，现有方法在相位选择上依赖数值扫描，且对外部扰动鲁棒性不足。

Method: 第一阶段利用Clohessy-Wiltshire动力学，将转移成本参数化为相位角，通过四次多项式求根得到最优入轨相位；第二阶段采用自适应滑模控制器（ASMC）和滑模扰动观测器（SMDO），基于二阶滑模面抑制扰动，并利用参考速度/加速度信号实现实用导数估计。

Result: 仿真表明该方法能实现精确跟踪、有效扰动抑制和两阶段的平滑过渡。

Conclusion: 该框架在保证能量最优的同时增强了鲁棒性，适用于受扰环境下的编队飞行控制。

Abstract: This paper presents a two-phase relative orbit control framework for spacecraft formation flying that combines analytic energy-optimal transfer with robust adaptive sliding mode tracking. In the first phase, a chaser is transferred from an arbitrary initial relative state to a projected circular orbit (PCO) under the Clohessy--Wiltshire dynamics. Rather than selecting the PCO entry phase by numerical sweeping, the transfer cost is parameterized by the phase angle, and the stationarity condition is reduced to a quartic polynomial whose real roots yield all candidate entry phases. In the second phase, the chaser maintains the PCO in the presence of external disturbances. An adaptive sliding mode controller (ASMC) and a sliding mode disturbance observer (SMDO) are employed in both phases to provide robust tracking and disturbance compensation. The observer reduces the lumped disturbance to a bounded residual, while the controller updates its adaptive gain from an estimated-state second-order sliding variable. The second-order surface tightens the ultimate tracking error bound, and a practical derivative estimation method reuses available reference velocity and acceleration signals, avoiding finite-difference noise amplification and additional differentiator tuning. Simulations demonstrate accurate tracking, effective disturbance rejection, and a smooth transition between the two phases.

</details>


### [320] [Dynamics-matched Physical Reservoir Computing for Undersensed Traffic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27371)
*Michael McCreesh,Rohit Gupta,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出使用交通网络作为储备池（称为IIDM-RC）来预测欠感知车辆网络的行为，通过IIDM模型满足回声状态性质，并在预测精度和训练时间上优于ESN和LSTM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶等应用中的交通预测需要高精度和快速响应，现有方法（如LSTM）训练成本高，而储备计算以低训练成本著称，但如何将储备计算应用于交通网络仍需探索。

Method: 采用改进智能驾驶员模型（IIDM）作为储备池，对欠感知交通网络的动力学行为进行预测；理论上证明了该类输入满足回声状态性质，并在仿真中验证其对更广泛输入的适用性。

Result: IIDM-RC对服从不同跟驰模型的欠感知车辆网络预测准确，且训练时间显著优于ESN和LSTM。

Conclusion: 利用匹配的非线性动力学（交通网络本身）作为储备池可以实现高效、准确的预测，为交通预测提供了一种低计算成本的新方法。

Abstract: Machine learning methods are increasingly used for traffic prediction in applications such as autonomous driving. Such predictions must be both highly accurate and immediately available, making methods with low computational costs and fast training times of interest. One such method is reservoir computing, in which the rich dynamics of a nonlinear system serves as a computational substrate and only a linear readout vector is trained. In this work we use a traffic network as the reservoir for predicting the behavior of an undersensed traffic network. This matching of the highly nonlinear dynamics allows for similar encoding between the behaviors of the reservoir and target network, enabling a more direct prediction. We show that a reservoir governed by the Improved Intelligent Driver Model (IIDM) satisfies the echo state property for a class of slowly-varying inputs. Through simulations we show that the echo state property likely holds for a larger class of inputs, and that the IIDM reservoir computer (IIDM-RC) accurately predicts an undersensed vehicle network governed by varying car-following models. We also compare with echo state networks (ESNs) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, finding improvements using IIDM-RC in both prediction accuracy and training time.

</details>


### [321] [Inter-Area Oscillation Damping in Data-Center-Integrated Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27575)
*Ahmed Alfatlawi,Masoud H. Nazari*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文建立了超大规模数据中心（含HVAC和UPS子系统）的动态模型，并整合到小信号稳定性框架中，研究需求响应对电力系统区域间振荡的影响。结果表明，UPS需求响应能增强阻尼，而HVAC因热响应带宽有限无法有效阻尼。通过梯度优化算法调整UPS控制器增益，最大化关键区域间模式的阻尼比，并在IEEE 39节点系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心作为新型负荷，其需求响应潜力可能影响电力系统稳定性，但此前缺乏对数据中心内部子系统（如HVAC和UPS）动态建模及其对区域间振荡影响的量化研究。

Method: 建立了数据中心HVAC和UPS的显式动态模型，集成到小信号稳定性分析框架；采用特征值分析和时域仿真评估阻尼效果；使用基于梯度的优化算法调整UPS控制器增益。

Result: UPS需求响应能增强区域间振荡阻尼，而HVAC无法提供有效阻尼；优化后的UPS控制器增益显著提高了关键模式的阻尼比；IEEE 39节点系统验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: UPS需求响应可作为改善电力系统区域间振荡阻尼的有效手段，而HVAC受限于热响应带宽不适合用于阻尼控制。通过优化UPS控制器参数可最大化阻尼效果。

Abstract: This paper develops explicit dynamic models of a hyperscale data center, including its heating, ventilation, and air conditioning (HVAC) and uninterruptible power supply (UPS) subsystems, and integrates them into a small-signal stability framework to investigate the impact of data center demand response on power system inter-area oscillations. Through eigenvalue analysis and time-domain simulations, the results demonstrate that UPS-based demand response can enhance inter-area oscillation damping. In contrast, the HVAC subsystem is shown to be inherently incapable of providing effective oscillation damping due to its limited thermal response bandwidth. A gradient-based optimization algorithm is used to tune the UPS controller gain to maximize the damping ratio of the critical inter-area mode. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is validated using the IEEE 39-bus test system.

</details>


### [322] [Chance-Constrained Nonlinear Covariance Control via Robust Linearization Remainder Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27742)
*Man Jun Koh,Hyochoong Bang,SooJean Han*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于序贯凸规划的离散时间协方差控制框架，通过将非线性映射转化为线性随机包含并利用鲁棒S-LMI传播二阶矩，解决了线性化截断导致的约束违反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典协方差控制对非线性系统使用一阶线性化传播不确定性，忽略高阶泰勒余项，导致统计矩不准确和机会约束违反。

Method: 将确定性一步非线性映射建模为线性随机包含，用无结构不确定块界定额外项；基于Petersen引理推导鲁棒S-LMI以传播二阶矩；使用马尔可夫迹不等式分析域退出风险，并用高斯单峰二阶矩界在差凸规划中施加空间机会约束。

Result: 在状态依赖的非线性动态系统仿真中验证了约束满足性。

Conclusion: 该SCP框架有效处理非线性系统中的不确定性传播与约束满足问题。

Abstract: When dealing with nonlinear systems, classical covariance steering typically propagates uncertainty via first-order linearizations, discarding higher-order Taylor remainders. This truncation causes computed statistical moments to diverge from the true physical state distribution, often leading to chance constraint violations. This paper introduces a discrete-time Sequential Convex Programming (SCP) framework that casts the deterministic one-step nonlinear numerical map as a Linear Stochastic Inclusion. The Taylor remainder is bounded within an unstructured uncertainty block over a uniform envelope. The second-moment tubes are propagated via what we refer to as a robust Stochastic Linear Matrix Inequality (S-LMI) derived from the Petersen's lemma, providing an upper bound on the expected uncentered second moment. Domain-exit risk is bounded analytically via a Markov trace inequality, and spatial chance constraints are enforced via Gauss unimodal second-moment bounds within a Difference-of-Convex program. Simulations on a state-dependent nonlinear dynamic system demonstrate constraint satisfaction.

</details>


### [323] [Robust PIDNet Control of a Dual-Actuator Thrust Vectoring Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27793)
*Connor Calme,Lundon Salley,AJ Munoz-Vazquez,Luis F. Zapata-Rivera*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于双执行器推力矢量平台姿态跟踪的鲁棒控制器，结合有界非线性PD控制与径向基函数（RBF）网络在线自适应补偿，实验验证其性能优于传统PID和超扭滑模控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制方法（如PID和超扭滑模）在处理双执行器推力矢量平台的交叉耦合、方向依赖摩擦和几何耦合时性能受限，需要模型无关的自适应补偿方案来提高跟踪精度。

Method: 采用有界非线性PD控制结合RBF神经网络，通过在线权重自适应生成状态相关的积分补偿，仅利用执行器位移测量得到的跟踪误差及其导数，形成耦合MIMO控制结构，并通过凸Lyapunov函数和H-infinity增益分析建立稳定性。

Result: 在二自由度万向节实验平台上，所提控制器相比传统PID和超扭滑模控制器具有更好的跟踪性能，在线自适应相比非自适应基线将ITNE降低10%，且无额外控制代价。

Conclusion: 该控制器实现了模型无关的鲁棒姿态跟踪，有效补偿了交叉通道影响，实验证明了其优越性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a robust controller for attitude tracking on a dual-actuator thrust vectoring platform. The control objective is to track a desired thrust direction on the unit sphere using two linear actuators connected through a universal joint. The proposed framework combines a bounded nonlinear PD action with a radial basis function (RBF) network that generates state-dependent integral compensation through online weight adaptation, relying solely on tracking error and its time derivative from actuator-displacement measurements. The control law is formulated as a coupled multiple-input--multiple-output structure, enabling the RBF network to compensate cross-channel effects, including direction-dependent friction and geometric coupling, with model-free implementation. Stability is established through a convex Lyapunov function whose gradient is naturally bounded along the error manifold. An H-infinity gain bound is derived for the extended error, with a local linearized interpretation in terms of tracking accuracy. The proposed controller is validated experimentally on a two-degree-of-freedom gimbal rig, achieving improved performance relative to a conventional PID and to a super-twisting controller, with online adaptation reducing the ITNE by 10% over the non-adaptive baseline at no additional control effort.

</details>


### [324] [Safety-Gated Agentic Supervisory Control on a Coupled Distillation Benchmark: Regime Map, Auditable Gate, and Co-Design Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27849)
*Christian Rosenthal*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于规则的分叉孪生反事实门控机制，用于约束LLM在化工过程控制中的设定值建议，在保持底层控制不变的情况下，显著提升异常工况下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够频繁生成设定值，但缺乏硬性约束检查，可能在异常工况下导致危险操作，需要一种可靠的监管机制。

Method: 设计规则驱动的分叉孪生反事实门控，包含9个固定约束，并在监管层前执行接受/阻止决策；对比PID、线性MPC、无门控代理和有门控代理四种控制策略。

Result: 在名义目标跟踪中，门控代理（C3）的IAE比线性MPC（C1）低（比值0.361）；但在扰动抑制中，无门控LLM表现不佳（比值16.03），而门控显著减少规范背离（IAE从11.5降至0.77）；在250个单元测试中，534/590次门控干预属于边界几何问题，有效阻止了318个有害建议。

Conclusion: 门控机制能有效约束LLM在化工控制中的行为，避免异常情况下的危险操作，但性能高度依赖于模型和场景，需谨慎推广。

Abstract: An open-weight LLM can write composition setpoints every five minutes. What a plant still needs is a hard check: named constraints, logged margins, and an admit/block decision before the regulatory layer moves. This paper puts that check in a rule-based forked-twin counterfactual gate (nine pinned constraints) and leaves the regulatory layer unchanged. On Skogestad's Column A the ladder is PID-only (C0), linear MPC (C1), ungated agent (C2), and gated agent (C3) under one contract: identical level closure (M_D, M_B), scenarios, and seeds; C2/C3 share the linear-MPC backend.
  The split is not subtle. Off-nominal target acquisition: the agent beats Pareto-tuned linear MPC in the strong band (C2/C1 IAE ratio 0.361 at the upper CI). Disturbance rejection on the same 16-point grid inverts by 16.03 at the upper CI (10.18 at the point estimate), where an ungated LLM supervisor does not belong. The gate compresses a specification-abandonment attractor into a bounded offset (d approx. -1.4; P95 cell IAE 11.5 to 0.77). A one-line prompt fix removes the attractor at source (6/10 to 0/10; sensitivity only, not a new headline). In a 250-cell statistical pass, 534 of 590 gate interventions are spec-on-bound geometry: the operating specification sits on a safety limit, so a well-behaved OP becomes inoperable while misbehaving ones are only contained; 318 blocks still correct actively harmful proposals.
  Headlines are single-column and model-conditional on DeepSeek-V4-Flash. A second-family sweep (NVIDIA Nemotron-3-Super) keeps the disturbance-rejection fails band and plant-side failure geography; magnitudes and protocol operability stay model-conditional, and Super target-acquisition strong cells are survivors only (not confirmation). Transfer means twin, constraint envelope, and setpoint interface, not a second plant class measured here.

</details>


### [325] [On the Strong Structural Controllability of Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27852)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体网络的强结构可控性，通过矩阵空间基分解和分层距离划分，提出了更紧的可控子空间上下界，并设计了多项式时间算法来求解最优基，最后验证了强结构可观性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多假设网络权重为标量或对称矩阵，难以处理矩阵奇异性和非对称耦合，且可控子空间的数学边界不够精确，缺乏有效的自动化算法。

Method: 提出了矩阵空间基分解方法，将矩阵加权网络转化为分层标量网络；引入分层距离划分（LDP）和挤压定理以收紧上下界；基于零空间投影设计代数算法寻找最优基；利用模式矩阵和通用秩证明最优基的几乎处处存在性；提出基于多层WL颜色细化的多项式时间自动发现算法。

Result: 建立了强结构可控子空间的上界和下界，通过分层距离划分使边界更紧；证明了最优基在参数空间中几乎处处存在；数值例子验证了理论，并评估了强结构可观性。

Conclusion: 本文为矩阵加权多智能体网络的强结构可控性提供了更精确的数学刻画和高效算法，拓展了经典结果，并揭示了网络拓扑与结构可控性的深层联系。

Abstract: This paper investigates the strong structural controllability of multi-agent networks. Based on the definition of equitable partitions, an upper bound for the strong structural controllable subspace (SSCS) is established. To reflect the physical significance of matrix weights where the state dimension is greater than one, the multi-agent system is modeled using higher-order dynamics. Furthermore, to address matrix singularity and asymmetric couplings, a matrix space basis decomposition method is proposed to transform the matrix-weighted network into layered scalar networks. Additionally, by extending this basis decomposition to the lower bound estimation, a layer-specific distance partition (LDP) is introduced. This formulation establishes a tighter Squeeze Theorem, narrowing the mathematical boundaries for the controllable subspace by capturing layer-specific structural delays. To systematically identify the optimal basis that minimizes the bounds gap, an algebraic algorithm based on null-space projection is formulated. Furthermore, by introducing pattern matrices and generic rank, the almost-everywhere existence of this optimal basis in the parameter space is rigorously proved, perfectly aligning with the definition of strong structural controllability. To break the NP-hard combinatorial bottleneck of manually pre-defining the targets, a polynomial-time automated discovery algorithm based on the multi-layer Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) color refinement is proposed. Finally, the strong structural observability and invariant attributes of the network are evaluated. Numerical examples with asymmetric matrix weights and directed multi-layer topologies are provided to verify the derived theorems.

</details>


### [326] [Data-Driven Formal Methods for Complex Dynamical Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27908)
*Behrad Samari,Alessandro Abate,Antoine Girard,Majid Zamani,Amy Nejati,Abolfazl Lavaei*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了具有形式保证的数据驱动方法，用于确定性及随机动态系统的验证与控制器合成，按三种方法论支柱和三类保证进行分类，并强调了随机系统的特有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动形式化方法文献分散、缺乏统一组织，难以理解其原理、区别和应用潜力，因此需要系统性综述。

Method: 围绕形式化方法的三大方法论支柱（无限/有限抽象、函数证书、组合方法）组织文献，并将数据驱动保证分为三类：统计保证（PAC和场景方法）、基于Lipschitz连续性的保证、利用结构性质的保证。

Result: 提供了全面的综述框架，分类了数百篇文章，突出确定性系统研究更丰富，并指出随机系统相比确定性的差异和挑战。

Conclusion: 填补了该领域缺乏系统性综述的空白，为从业者理解数据驱动形式化方法提供了结构化的视角，并指出了随机系统方向的开放问题。

Abstract: Data-driven approaches with formal guarantees have recently emerged as a powerful means for the verification and controller synthesis of complex dynamical systems. Interest in these methods is rapidly growing, as system models are often unavailable in practice, and challenges such as nonlinear behavior, uncertainty, and the curse of dimensionality typically render accurate modeling infeasible. These difficulties motivate leveraging limited data collected from the system while still providing formal guarantees on its overall behavior. The community has therefore proposed a few hundred articles on the development of data-driven frameworks that enable the formal verification and synthesis of dynamical systems without explicit models, addressing complex specifications beyond stability. Despite this rapid growth, existing results remain scattered and lack a coherent organization, limiting a clear understanding of their principles, distinctions, and practical potential. This survey fills this gap by providing a comprehensive overview of these data-driven methods for both deterministic and stochastic dynamical systems. We structure the literature around three main methodological pillars in formal methods: (in)finite-abstraction-based techniques, functional certificate approaches, such as control barrier certificates, and compositional methods. For each of these approaches, we classify the resulting data-driven guarantees into three main categories: (i) statistical guarantees grounded in probably approximately correct and scenario-based frameworks, (ii) guarantees derived from Lipschitz continuity, and (iii) guarantees exploiting structural properties. While the literature on deterministic systems is considerably richer, we also devote particular attention to the stochastic counterpart, highlighting the inherent differences and challenges that arise compared to the deterministic case.

</details>


### [327] [Projection-Regularized Indirect Data-Driven Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28123)
*Mahmood Mazare,Hossein Ramezani*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为投影正则化预测控制（PRPC）的新方法，通过正则化投影和协方差更新，在噪声和数据稀缺条件下提高了预测精度，并实现了对线性时变系统的自适应控制，同时保证了安全性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接数据驱动预测控制方法在处理过程噪声和数据稀缺时表现不佳，导致预测误差增大和控制性能下降。为此，需要一种能够有效应对这些问题的新方法。

Method: PRPC通过将基本引理权重向量进行正则化投影，并转化为固定维度的协方差更新，结合偏差-方差分析，在过程噪声和结构秩不足情况下减少预测误差。此外，采用滑动窗口自适应控制器，并利用向量值鞅浓度不等式推导出预测器的有限样本置信界，进而动态收紧约束集，以确保鲁棒可行性和输入-状态实际稳定性。

Result: 在LTI和LTV基准测试上的仿真表明，PRPC具有实时计算效率和严格的约束满足性，同时在噪声条件下显著降低了预测误差。

Conclusion: PRPC提供了一种在噪声和数据稀缺条件下有效且安全的预测控制方案，适用于线性时变系统，并具有理论保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: Indirect data-driven predictive control methods often suffer under process noise and data scarcity. This paper introduces Projection-Regularized Predictive Control (PRPC), retaining the fundamental-lemma weight vector via a regularized projection analytically condensed into an efficient, fixed-dimension covariance update. A rigorous bias--variance analysis proves PRPC strictly reduces prediction error under process noise (errors-in-variables) and structural rank deficiencies compared to unregularized subspace methods. We leverage these properties to develop an adaptive sliding-window controller for linear time-varying (LTV) systems. To guarantee safety despite closed-loop data correlations, we derive a uniform-in-time, finite-sample confidence bound on the empirical predictor using vector-valued martingale concentration inequalities. Embedding this statistical uncertainty radius into a dynamically tightened constraint set rigorously ensures robust recursive feasibility and Input-to-State practical Stability (ISpS) with high probability. Simulations on LTI and LTV benchmarks demonstrate real-time tractability and strict constraint satisfaction.

</details>


### [328] [Data-Driven Dead-Zone Compensation via Projection in Predictive Control Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28142)
*Mahmood Mazare,Hossein Ramezani*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种无模型、无参数化的数据驱动架构，通过绝对子空间预测器识别执行器死区，利用正交投影在线补偿，实现无偏移跟踪，并在谐振系统和电力电子设备上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器死区是运动控制中常见的非线性问题，导致稳态偏移或极限环，现有方法需要模型或参数化，且复杂动态估计器或探测信号不实用。

Method: 结合速度形式预测器和绝对子空间预测器，后者作为数据驱动稳态传感器，通过残差作为代理嵌入行为Hankel矩阵，将死区估计简化为固定正交投影，集成于子空间预测控制器。

Result: 框架递归可行且实际输入-状态稳定，在六阶欠阻尼多自由度扭转系统上实现±0.18V死区的无偏移跟踪，并在高精度功率放大器上有效抑制死区时间非线性。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动方法无需模型或死区参数化，实现实时补偿，适用于运动控制和电力电子，具有实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Actuator dead-zones are a common and troublesome nonlinearity in motion control: a band of commanded effort over which the plant does not respond, leaving a steady-state offset or a limit cycle. This paper proposes a data-driven architecture that compensates such mismatches without a model of the plant and without any parameterization of the dead-zone. The central idea is to identify, alongside the velocity-form predictor used for control, a second absolute subspace predictor. Because the absolute predictor carries no integral action, it behaves as a data-driven steady-state sensor, so a persistent actuator mismatch appears as a proportional prediction residual. Embedding this residual as a proxy in a behavioral Hankel matrix reduces the mismatch estimate to a single fixed orthogonal projection evaluated online, with no dynamic estimator, no injected probing signal, and no run-time prediction-error computation. Integrated into a subspace predictive controller, the framework is shown to be recursively feasible and practically input-to-state stable, and it recovers offset-free tracking once the dead-band traversal settles. The approach is validated in real time on a sixth-order, lightly damped Quanser multi-DOF torsion system, whose complex-conjugate poles give a lightly damped open-loop response, achieving offset-free tracking across a $\pm 0.18$\,V actuator dead-band. A second study on a high-precision power amplifier shows that the same architecture rejects dead-time-induced nonlinearities in fast-switching power electronics.

</details>


### [329] [Stochastic Average Consensus Filtering and Distributed State Estimation for Boolean Control Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28158)
*Rong Yang,Chi Huang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对布尔控制网络（BCNs）提出了一种分布式多传感器融合状态估计与一致性滤波方法，通过概率测度变换、半张量积和随机逼近技术，实现了基于局部通信的全局状态估计，并证明了算法的几乎必然收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的集中式多传感器估计方案存在通信成本高和单点故障的缺陷，而连续状态的一致性算法难以直接扩展到离散逻辑系统，因此需要一种适用于BCNs的分布式估计方法。

Method: 结合概率测度变换、半张量积和随机逼近，设计了分布式随机平均一致性滤波器，并利用鞅收敛定理和摄动随机Lyapunov函数证明了算法的几乎必然收敛性。

Result: 提出的框架通过局部通信实现了全局状态估计，避免了集中式架构的缺点，并证明了算法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该研究为BCNs提供了一种有效的分布式状态估计方法，克服了集中式方案的局限，具有理论保证和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper addresses the distributed multi-sensor fusion state estimation and consensus filtering for Boolean control networks (BCNs). Existing centralized multi-sensor estimation schemes for stochastic BCNs have limitations of high communication costs and single-point failures, and continuous-state consensus algorithms are difficult to extend to discrete logical systems. By integrating probability measure transformation, semi-tensor product and stochastic approximation, a distributed stochastic average consensus filter is proposed. Moreover, the almost sure convergence of the algorithm is proved by martingale convergence theorem and perturbed stochastic Lyapunov functions. The proposed framework realizes global state estimation via local communication, avoiding the defects of centralized architectures.

</details>


### [330] [Self-Evolving Learning for Embodied AI with Criticality Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28251)
*Linxuan He,Yuying Tian,Lingxiang Fan,Jiaqi Pi,Yinqiao Lu,Shang Su,Mengkai Shi,Shuo Feng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种自演化方法，通过状态关键性模型引导重要性采样，用失败场景替换冗余常规场景，打破具身AI系统微调时的性能平台期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI系统在任务微调时表现停滞，原因是默认数据收集随机处理所有样本，导致数据集被常规场景主导，遗漏了最有价值的失败案例。

Method: 学习一个状态关键性模型预测未来失败概率，引导重要性采样偏向失败易发场景；在训练中通过重要性权重重采样数据，保持无偏目标并提高信息密度。

Result: 在四足运动、多任务操作、视觉-语言-行动基准和真实机器人任务中，失败率相对基准降低51-67%，相对SOTA VLA模型降低8-25%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效破解微调平台期，通过聚焦失败场景提升数据效率，显著降低失败率并优于现有方法。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in policy pretraining, embodied AI systems routinely plateau during task-specific finetuning. The root cause lies in how finetuning data are collected: the default pipeline gathers data randomly, treating every sample as informative. Datasets become dominated by nominal scenarios, while rare failure cases--the most valuable for improvement--are missed. We propose a self-evolving method that breaks this plateau. Our core insight is that a state-wise criticality model, learned from the policy's own execution outcomes to predict the probability of future failure, can guide importance sampling toward failure-prone scenarios. After replacing redundant nominal scenarios with diverse failure-prone ones, importance weights are used to resample the data during training. This effectively preserves an unbiased learning objective while fundamentally increasing the information density of the training pool. Across quadrupedal locomotion, multi-task manipulation, vision-language-action benchmarks, and a real-robot task, our method reduces failure rates by 51--67% relative to trained baselines and by 8-25% relative to state-of-the-art vision-language-action models.

</details>


### [331] [Grid-Forming Converter DC-link Control Considering the Primary Energy Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28270)
*Carlo de Paolis Robles,Andrés Tomás-Martín,Ignacio Egido,Aurelio García-Cerrada*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种考虑电网形成变流器直流侧动态特性的控制参数整定方法，通过遗传算法优化控制参数，以降低暂态功率失衡时变流器脱网风险并增强系统韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电网形成变流器直流链路控制大多假设直流电源理想且无约束，忽略了原动机的动态响应和运行限制，可能导致暂态过程中直流侧功率不足，增加变流器脱网风险。

Method: 提出一种基于遗传算法的系统化控制参数整定方法，在直流链路控制设计中显式建模原动机动态和直流侧功率约束，并通过详细电磁暂态仿真验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的控制方法在突然功率失衡时能降低变流器从电网脱网的风险，并增强系统韧性。

Conclusion: 将原动机动态和直流侧功率约束纳入直流链路控制设计，可提高电网形成变流器在暂态过程中的稳定性，增强电力系统韧性。
